# Prompt pixel art 4 mặt cho sprite stacking — 144 lính (12 hệ × 12 lính)

Bản chuyển từ `docs/art/unit-prompts.md` sang **bảng 4 mặt pixel art** theo skill
[sprite-stacking](https://github.com/danielwh2/sprite-stacking): ảnh ra là một hàng **TOP, SIDE, FRONT, BACK** trên nền trong suốt,
đưa thẳng vào `views_to_slices.py` (không cần tách nền) để khắc thành dải lát (slice strip) rồi render giả 3D xoay được.

- **Tỉ lệ, vũ khí, khiên, thú cưỡi, máy** lấy thẳng từ lõi game (`packages/core`, qua `docs/art/unit-data.mjs`): đổi trong game thì chạy lại
  `python3 docs/art/gen_pixel_prompts.py .` (cần Node ≥ 23). Màu / tóc hệ, tên tiếng Anh, kiểu vải lấy ở `unit-prompts.md`.
- **Khung pixel theo ×scale từng lính** (lính thường ×1 = cao 36 px; kỵ binh, máy, khổng lồ có mốc riêng); số lát khắc = chiều cao.
- Lính 1–4 (Quang: thợ mỏ, kiếm sĩ, cung thủ, thích khách) giữ nguyên prompt bản trước vì đang vẽ dở.

## Khác gì so với prompt cũ

| Prompt cũ (turnaround anime) | Prompt pixel 4 mặt | Vì sao |
|---|---|---|
| 3 góc FRONT / TOP-DOWN / RIGHT SIDE | 4 góc **TOP, SIDE, FRONT, BACK**, 1 hàng | Slicer cần đủ 4 hình chiếu, đúng thứ tự |
| Nền xám có chữ nhãn + đường kẻ ngang | **Nền trong suốt** (PNG alpha), không chữ, không đường kẻ | Khỏi bước tách nền; chữ / đường kẻ bị khắc thành khối rác |
| SIDE quay phải | **SIDE quay trái**, TOP mặt quay **xuống** | Quy ước của slicer |
| Glow, bloom mềm, bụi sáng bay quanh | Phát sáng = cụm pixel đặc, hiệu ứng **dính vào thân / vũ khí** | Viền mềm bị nhoè khi xoay, hạt rời khắc ra rác |
| Tóc dựng to, cao | Tóc **vừa phải**, cao khoảng 1/3 đầu, ôm sát đầu | Tóc cao làm nhân vật mất cân đối, khi khắc ra thành cột lát mỏng |
| Tay chân, dây cung mảnh | Tối thiểu **2-3 px** | Phần 1 px nhấp nháy khi xoay |
| Áo choàng bay ngang, vũ khí chĩa xa | Áo choàng ôm sau lưng, vũ khí sát thân | Khối lồi ra làm stack quay lệch tâm |
| Thủy: thân nước trong suốt | Màu đặc, không trong suốt | Phần bán trong suốt bị khoét thủng khi khắc |

## Cách dùng

1. Copy prompt, **không cần** đính kèm ảnh mẫu (nếu đính kèm thì chỉ để lấy màu / kiểu tóc, không lấy góc nhìn).
2. Lưu ảnh vào `docs/art/pixel/<mã hệ>/NN-<loại>-views.png` (tên ghi cạnh từng prompt).
3. Xem ảnh trước khi khắc: đúng 4 hình, **nền trong suốt thật** (không phải ô caro vẽ giả), TOP mặt quay xuống, SIDE quay trái, cùng chiều cao, nét pixel sắc. Sai thì tạo lại, đừng cố cứu.
   - Công cụ nào không xuất được nền trong suốt sẽ hay **vẽ ô caro xám-trắng giả** vào ảnh: ô caro đó slicer không tách được, phải tạo lại. ChatGPT (tạo ảnh) xuất PNG trong suốt thật; nếu công cụ vẫn ra nền trắng phẳng thì slicer vẫn tự tách được.
4. Khắc (lệnh ghi cạnh từng prompt), mở `check.png` xem ngay:

```bash
S=.claude/skills/sprite-stacking/scripts   # skill nằm sẵn trong repo
uv run --with numpy --with pillow python3 $S/views_to_slices.py docs/art/pixel/light/02-sword-views.png docs/art/pixel/light/02-sword-slices.png \
  --slices 36 --top-nose down --emissive docs/art/pixel/light/02-sword-glow.png \
  --preview docs/art/pixel/light/02-sword-check.png --html docs/art/pixel/light/02-sword.html
```

- `--top-nose down` luôn bật vì lính không phải xe (auto sẽ lật ngược).
- `--emissive` tách mặt nạ phần phát sáng (mắt, vũ khí) để renderer cộng glow lúc chạy thay vì vẽ cứng vào ảnh.
- Ảnh quá to (AI hay xuất 1024 px+) vẫn khắc được: `--slices` quyết định độ chi tiết, không phải độ phân giải file.
- Lính đổi tư thế (đánh, chạy) cần thêm bảng 4 mặt cho từng frame, cùng kích thước và cùng số lát (xem Lessons của skill).

**Animation đứng yên:** dưới mỗi lính có thêm prompt *Đứng yên* — một ảnh 3 hàng (ngang quay trái / trước / sau) × 4 khung
thở lặp lại (thân nhún 1 px, tóc và vải lay, chỗ phát sáng nhịp sáng, chuyển động phụ riêng từng loại lính: thú cưỡi gõ móng
vẫy đuôi, cờ bay, quả cầu phép nhịp sáng…). Đính kèm bảng 4 mặt của chính lính đó làm mẫu. Demo thế giới đọc ảnh gộp này qua
rig `anim.idle` (`sheet` + `order`) trong `docs/art/topdown/rigs.json`.

| Hệ | Số | Thư mục |

| 5. ⚡ Lôi Kiếm Các — Thunder Blade Hall (hệ Lôi) | 49–60 | `docs/art/pixel/thunder/` |
| 6. 🩸 Huyết Nguyệt Giáo — Blood Moon Cult (hệ Huyết) | 61–72 | `docs/art/pixel/blood/` |
| 7. 💎 Tinh Thạch Cốc — Crystal Vale (hệ Tinh thể) | 73–84 | `docs/art/pixel/crystal/` |
| 8. 🪨 Bàn Thạch Bảo — Stone Bastion (hệ Thạch) | 85–96 | `docs/art/pixel/stone/` |

## 1. ☀️ Thánh Quang Điện — Temple of Light (hệ Quang)

**1. Thợ mỏ** (×1) → lưu thành `docs/art/pixel/light/01-miner-views.png` · khắc `--slices 36 --top-nose down`

```
Chunky pixel art orthographic model sheet for sprite stacking (fake-3D rotatable sprite), rich 3-tone hue-shifted pixel shading (deep cool-shifted shadows, saturated midtones, bright warm top highlights along edges), solid flat colour clusters. Four views of the SAME character in one clean 1x4 horizontal row on a fully TRANSPARENT background (PNG with alpha channel: every pixel outside the character is fully transparent, no background colour, no white, no checkerboard pattern, no frame), generous empty space between views, in this exact order left to right: 1. TOP view looking straight down at the head and shoulders, the character's front (face side) pointing DOWN. 2. SIDE view, the character facing LEFT. 3. FRONT view. 4. BACK view. Strictly orthographic: zero perspective, zero tilt, zero 3/4 angle. Identical pixel scale in all four views: the height in SIDE, FRONT and BACK is the same, the width in TOP equals the width in FRONT and BACK, the length in TOP equals the length in SIDE. Character centred in every view, standing upright on one shared ground line. Native grid: about 20 x 20 px footprint, about 36 px tall, every art pixel a crisp square block, then upscaled with nearest neighbour. Character: stickman with a dark body tinted with the element colour, round head, simple round hands, no face except two sharp slanted glowing eyes drawn as 2-pixel clusters with a white core pixel. Arms, legs, weapon shafts, bow strings and staffs at least 2-3 pixels thick, never 1-pixel lines. Medium-length element hair as bold pixel clusters, close to the head: it rises only about a third of the head height above the skull (about 3-4 art pixels), never tall, towering or taller than the head. Cloth (scarf and/or cape) in one single strong colour with stepped ragged pixel edges, hanging close behind the body with the same shape in all four views. Weapon and magic glow drawn as solid bright pixel clusters with a white-hot core, never soft bloom. Every element effect (sparks, motes, rays, smoke, petals, droplets, flames) is a solid pixel shape touching the body or weapon, nothing floating loose. Keep the weapon close to the body so the silhouette stays compact and centred; every gap between limbs, weapon and body opens to the outside of the silhouette, no fully enclosed holes. Bold 1px dark outline around each view, zero anti-aliasing, zero soft edges, hard alpha edges (each pixel fully opaque or fully transparent). Element: Light (Temple of Light). Head: short-to-medium golden spiky hair with a glowing tip, flicking up a little like small flames. Hair stays medium height, about a third of the head height above the skull, never tall. Body: black silhouette with a strong golden rim glow, golden sparks crackling around the fists, glittering gold motes. Cloth colour: rich gold (#e8a91c), long scarf flowing into a cape. Eyes, weapon and magic glow: radiant gold (#ffd84a) with light rays and glittering motes. (Colours and hair/body motif only; draw every effect as solid attached pixel clusters.) Unit: gold miner "Miner". Cloth: short scarf. Gear: glowing pickaxe. Pose: tool on the shoulder, relaxed. Avoid: white background, coloured background, checkerboard pattern, fake transparency grid, drop shadow, semi-transparent edges, perspective, 3/4 view, isometric, labels, text, letters, guide lines, grid lines, shadows on the ground, glow halo, bloom, blur, gradients, anti-aliasing, 1-pixel thin lines, floating particles, painterly, realistic, 3d render, detailed face, mouth, nose, skin, muscles, bulky armour, extra limbs, multiple cloth colours, background scenery, cropped feet, views with different sizes or poses, watermark.
```

*Đứng yên — Thợ mỏ* → đính kèm `docs/art/pixel/light/01-miner-views.png`, lưu thành `docs/art/pixel/light/01-miner-idle.png` (3 hàng × 4 khung)

```
Pixel art sprite sheet: a looping 4-frame IDLE (standing still, breathing) animation of the SAME unit as in the attached 4-view model sheet (same colours, hair, cloth, gear and pixel scale). Three rows, one view per row, top to bottom: row 1 SIDE view facing LEFT, row 2 FRONT view, row 3 BACK view (no face visible). Four frames per row, left to right in time order, the loop returning seamlessly from frame 4 to frame 1. Fully TRANSPARENT background (PNG alpha: no background colour, no white, no checkerboard pattern), generous equal empty space between all frames and rows (no frame touching another), every frame the same canvas size, the feet on one shared ground line at the same spot in every frame of a row. Strictly orthographic, zero perspective, zero 3/4 angle. Native grid: about 18 x 18 px footprint, about 32 px tall, every art pixel a crisp square block, upscaled with nearest neighbour, bold 1px dark outline, zero anti-aliasing, hard alpha edges. Subtle motion only: the body and head rise and fall by 1 pixel with the breath, hair and cloth sway 1-2 pixels, glowing parts pulse slightly brighter and back; feet never move and the silhouette stays the same size. Frames: 1. neutral. 2. inhale: chest and head 1 px up, cloth lifting. 3. neutral, the free hand wipes the brow then drops back. 4. exhale: chest and head 1 px down, cloth settling. Element: Light (Temple of Light). Head: short-to-medium golden spiky hair with a glowing tip, flicking up a little like small flames. Hair stays medium height, about a third of the head height above the skull, never tall. Body: black silhouette with a strong golden rim glow, golden sparks crackling around the fists, glittering gold motes. Cloth colour: rich gold (#e8a91c), long scarf flowing into a cape. Eyes, weapon and magic glow: radiant gold (#ffd84a) with light rays and glittering motes. (Colours and hair/body motif only; draw every effect as solid attached pixel clusters.) Unit: gold miner "Miner". Slightly smaller and leaner, about 90% of a normal stickman. Cloth: short scarf. Gear: glowing pickaxe. Pose: tool resting on the shoulder in one hand, relaxed. Avoid: white background, coloured background, checkerboard pattern, fake transparency grid, ground shadow, motion blur, speed lines, walking, attacking, big pose changes, the unit changing size, frames of different sizes, perspective, 3/4 view, the side view facing right, 1-pixel thin lines, anti-aliasing, glow halo, bloom, blur, gradients, floating particles, labels, text, frame numbers, borders, watermark.
```

**4. Thích khách ánh chớp** (×0.95) → lưu thành `docs/art/pixel/light/04-ninja-views.png` · khắc `--slices 32 --top-nose down`

```
Chunky pixel art orthographic model sheet for sprite stacking (fake-3D rotatable sprite), rich 3-tone hue-shifted pixel shading (deep cool-shifted shadows, saturated midtones, bright warm top highlights along edges), solid flat colour clusters. TOP view looking straight down at the head and shoulders, the character's front (face side) pointing DOWN. 2. SIDE view, the character facing LEFT. 3. FRONT view. 4. BACK view. Identical pixel scale in all four views: the height in SIDE, FRONT and BACK is the same, the width in TOP equals the width in FRONT and BACK, the length in TOP equals the length in SIDE. Character centred in every view, standing upright on one shared ground line. Native grid: about 16 x 16 px footprint, about 29 px tall, every art pixel a crisp square block, then upscaled with nearest neighbour. Character: stickman with a dark body tinted with the element colour, round head, simple round hands, no face except two sharp slanted glowing eyes drawn as 2-pixel clusters with a white core pixel. Arms, legs, weapon shafts, bow strings and staffs at least 2-3 pixels thick, never 1-pixel lines. Medium-length element hair as bold pixel clusters, close to the head: it rises only about a third of the head height above the skull (about 3-4 art pixels), never tall, towering or taller than the head. Cloth (scarf and/or cape) in one single strong colour with stepped ragged pixel edges, hanging close behind the body with the same shape in all four views. Weapon and magic glow drawn as solid bright pixel clusters with a white-hot core, never soft bloom. Every element effect (sparks, motes, rays, smoke, petals, droplets, flames) is a solid pixel shape touching the body or weapon, nothing floating loose. Keep the weapon close to the body so the silhouette stays compact and centred; every gap between limbs, weapon and body opens to the outside of the silhouette, no fully enclosed holes. Bold 1px dark outline around each view, zero anti-aliasing, zero soft edges, hard alpha edges (each pixel fully opaque or fully transparent). Element: Wind (Gale Horde). Head: medium silver-white spiky hair swept back by the wind, lying close to the head. Hair stays medium height, about a third of the head height above the skull, never tall. Body: dark navy silhouette, white bandage wraps on the forearms and a white waist sash, small solid pale-cyan pixel wind wings attached to the back, a swirling whirlwind around the feet. Cloth colour: sky blue (#2f8fc4), torn scarf. Eyes, weapon and magic glow: pale icy cyan (#a8ecff) with swirling wind streaks and flying debris. (Colours and hair/body motif only; draw every effect as solid attached pixel clusters.) Unit: gold miner "Breeze miner". Small and lean, about 80% of a normal stickman. Cloth: short scarf. Gear: glowing pickaxe. Pose: tool resting on the shoulder in one hand, relaxed. Chunky pixel art orthographic model sheet for sprite stacking (fake-3D rotatable sprite), rich 3-tone hue-shifted pixel shading (deep cool-shifted shadows, saturated midtones, bright warm top highlights along edges), solid flat colour clusters. Four views of the SAME character in one clean 1x4 horizontal row on a fully TRANSPARENT background (PNG with alpha channel: every pixel outside the character is fully transparent, no background colour, no white, no checkerboard pattern, no frame), generous empty space between views, in this exact order left to right: 1. TOP view looking straight down at the head and shoulders, the character's front (face side) pointing DOWN. 2. SIDE view, the character facing LEFT. 3. FRONT view. 4. BACK view. Strictly orthographic: zero perspective, zero tilt, zero 3/4 angle. Identical pixel scale in all four views: the height in SIDE, FRONT and BACK is the same, the width in TOP equals the width in FRONT and BACK, the length in TOP equals the length in SIDE. Character centred in every view, standing upright on one shared ground line. Native grid: about 20 x 20 px footprint, about 36 px tall, every art pixel a crisp square block, then upscaled with nearest neighbour. Character: stickman with a dark body tinted with the element colour, round head, simple round hands, no face except two sharp slanted glowing eyes drawn as 2-pixel clusters with a white core pixel. Arms, legs, weapon shafts, bow strings and staffs at least 2-3 pixels thick, never 1-pixel lines. Medium-length element hair as bold pixel clusters, close to the head: it rises only about a third of the head height above the skull (about 3-4 art pixels), never tall, towering or taller than the head. Cloth (scarf and/or cape) in one single strong colour with stepped ragged pixel edges, hanging close behind the body with the same shape in all four views. Weapon and magic glow drawn as solid bright pixel clusters with a white-hot core, never soft bloom. Every element effect (sparks, motes, rays, smoke, petals, droplets, flames) is a solid pixel shape touching the body or weapon, nothing floating loose. Keep the weapon close to the body so the silhouette stays compact and centred; every gap between limbs, weapon and body opens to the outside of the silhouette, no fully enclosed holes. Bold 1px dark outline around each view, zero anti-aliasing, zero soft edges, hard alpha edges (each pixel fully opaque or fully transparent). Element: Blood (Blood Moon Cult). Head: medium messy black-red spiky hair swept back, close to the head. Hair stays medium height, about a third of the head height above the skull, never tall. Body: very dark red-black silhouette with glowing red vein cracks on the torso and legs, blood-red glowing fists, curved blood slashes swirling around. Cloth colour: blood red (#9c0f1c), long torn scarf. Eyes, weapon and magic glow: vivid red (#ff2d3c) with flying blood droplets and crimson slash trails. (Colours and hair/body motif only; draw every effect as solid attached pixel clusters.) Unit: war musician (buffs allies) "Blood moon horn". Cloth: scarf and a flag of the same colour. Gear: a flag of the cloth colour on a pole strapped upright on the back, and a curved glowing war horn raised to the face. Pose: playing the instrument. For sprite stacking: The flag is a solid cloth panel at least 3 pixels thick, its pole upright beside or behind the body. Avoid: white background, coloured background, checkerboard pattern, fake transparency grid, drop shadow, semi-transparent edges, perspective, 3/4 view, isometric, labels, text, letters, guide lines, grid lines, shadows on the ground, glow halo, bloom, blur, gradients, anti-aliasing, 1-pixel thin lines, floating particles, painterly, realistic, 3d render, detailed face, mouth, nose, skin, muscles, bulky armour, extra limbs, multiple cloth colours, background scenery, cropped feet, views with different sizes or poses, watermark.

Four views of the SAME character in one clean 1x4 horizontal row on a fully TRANSPARENT background (PNG with alpha channel: every pixel outside the character is fully transparent, no background colour, no white, no checkerboard pattern, no frame), generous empty space between views, in this exact order left to right: 1. TOP view looking straight down at the head and shoulders, the character's front (face side) pointing DOWN. 2. SIDE view, the character facing LEFT. 3. FRONT view. 4. BACK view. Strictly orthographic: zero perspective, zero tilt, zero 3/4 angle. Identical pixel scale in all four views: the height in SIDE, FRONT and BACK is the same, the width in TOP equals the width in FRONT and BACK, the length in TOP equals the length in SIDE. Character centred in every view, standing upright on one shared ground line. Native grid: about 21 x 21 px footprint, about 38 px tall, every art pixel a crisp square block, then upscaled with nearest neighbour. Character: stickman with a dark body tinted with the element colour, round head, simple round hands, no face except two sharp slanted glowing eyes drawn as 2-pixel clusters with a white core pixel. Arms, legs, weapon shafts, bow strings and staffs at least 2-3 pixels thick, never 1-pixel lines. Medium-length element hair as bold pixel clusters, close to the head: it rises only about a third of the head height above the skull (about 3-4 art pixels), never tall, towering or taller than the head. Cloth (scarf and/or cape) in one single strong colour with stepped ragged pixel edges, hanging close behind the body with the same shape in all four views. Weapon and magic glow drawn as solid bright pixel clusters with a white-hot core, never soft bloom. Every element effect (sparks, motes, rays, smoke, petals, droplets, flames) is a solid pixel shape touching the body or weapon, nothing floating loose. Keep the weapon close to the body so the silhouette stays compact and centred; every gap between limbs, weapon and body opens to the outside of the silhouette, no fully enclosed holes. Bold 1px dark outline around each view, zero anti-aliasing, zero soft edges, hard alpha edges (each pixel fully opaque or fully transparent). Element: Ice (Frost Palace). Head: medium spiky light-blue ice-crystal hair with white glints, close to the head. Hair stays medium height, about a third of the head height above the skull, never tall. Body: dark blue-black silhouette armoured with jagged glowing ice crystals on chest, shoulders, forearms and shins, small ice shards floating. Cloth colour: deep ice blue (#2b67b8), torn cape. Eyes, weapon and magic glow: icy white-blue (#bfeaff) with ice shards and cold mist. (Colours and hair/body motif only; draw every effect as solid attached pixel clusters.) Unit: battle mage "Frost mage". Slightly bigger and sturdier, about 105% of a normal stickman. Cloth: hooded cloak. Gear: glowing staff in one hand, the element spell as a solid pixel orb in the other palm. Pose: spell hand thrust forward. Avoid: white background, coloured background, checkerboard pattern, fake transparency grid, drop shadow, semi-transparent edges, perspective, 3/4 view, isometric, labels, text, letters, guide lines, grid lines, shadows on the ground, glow halo, bloom, blur, gradients, anti-aliasing, 1-pixel thin lines, floating particles, painterly, realistic, 3d render, detailed face, mouth, nose, skin, muscles, bulky armour, extra limbs, multiple cloth colours, background scenery, cropped feet, views with different sizes or poses, watermark.
